// Display-only bilingual reconstruction. Never changes a log or saved state.

function koLogReference179(e,t){const tr=koOriginalTr179;return t&&Vse.has(t)?t:tr.sex.test(e)?"sex":tr.intimate.test(e)?"intimate":tr.flirt.test(e)?"flirt":tr.rude.test(e)?"rude":tr.gold.test(e)?"gold":tr.kind.test(e)?"kind":tr.menial.test(e)?"menial":tr.system.test(e)?"system":tr.thought.test(e)?"thought":"neutral";}
function koLogInit179(){
 if(koLogInit179.data)return koLogInit179.data;
 const exact=new Map(),patterns=[],english=[],englishSeen=new Set(),owners=new Map(),escape=s=>s.replace(/[.*+?^${}()|[\]\\]/g,'\\$&');
 const token=/⟦\d+⟧|\{[A-Za-z_$][\w.$]*\}|%[pwr]/g;
 // Raw stored particles and already rendered historical particles are equivalent.
 const literal=s=>s.split(/(은\(는\)|이\(가\)|을\(를\)|와\(과\)|으로\(로\)|\(으\)로)/g).map(x=>({'은(는)':'(?:은\\(는\\)|은|는)','이(가)':'(?:이\\(가\\)|이|가)','을(를)':'(?:을\\(를\\)|을|를)','와(과)':'(?:와\\(과\\)|와|과)','으로(로)':'(?:으로\\(로\\)|으로|로)','(으)로':'(?:\\(으\\)로|으로|로)'}[x]??escape(x))).join('');
 for(const row of koLogData179.forms){
  owners.set(row.id,row.owner);
  if(!englishSeen.has(row.en)){
   englishSeen.add(row.en);const pieces=row.en.split(token),anchor=pieces.slice().sort((a,b)=>b.length-a.length)[0]??'';
   if(anchor.length>=8)english.push({anchor,pattern:new RegExp('^'+pieces.map(escape).join('[\\s\\S]*?')+'$')});
  }
  const matches=[...row.ko.matchAll(token)];
  if(!matches.length){if(!exact.has(row.ko))exact.set(row.ko,[]);exact.get(row.ko).push(row);continue;}
  let at=0;const parts=[],anchors=[],tokens=[];
  for(const m of matches){const fixed=row.ko.slice(at,m.index);parts.push(literal(fixed),'([\\s\\S]*?)');anchors.push(...fixed.split(/은\(는\)|이\(가\)|을\(를\)|와\(과\)|으로\(로\)|\(으\)로/));tokens.push(m[0]);at=m.index+m[0].length;}
  const fixed=row.ko.slice(at);parts.push(literal(fixed));anchors.push(...fixed.split(/은\(는\)|이\(가\)|을\(를\)|와\(과\)|으로\(로\)|\(으\)로/));
  const anchor=anchors.sort((a,b)=>b.length-a.length)[0]?.trim()??'';
  // A near-empty template must not swallow arbitrary names or unrelated logs.
  if(anchor.length<5)continue;
  patterns.push({row,tokens,anchor,pattern:new RegExp('^'+parts.join('')+'$'),prefixes:(koLogData179.joins??[]).filter(j=>j.left.includes(row.owner)).map(j=>({join:j,pattern:new RegExp('^'+parts.join('')+'(?='+escape(j.separator)+')')}))});
 }
 return koLogInit179.data={exact,patterns,english,owners,token,cache:new Map()};
}
function koLogResolve179(text,state){
 const data=koLogInit179(),names=new Set();
 for(const c of Object.values(state?.characters??{}))if(c?.name)names.add(c.name);
 for(const c of [...(state?.customers??[]),...(state?.patronRecords??[])])if(c?.name)names.add(c.name);
 for(const name of Object.values(state?.namedPatronNames??{}))if(typeof name==='string')names.add(name);
 if(state?.player?.name)names.add(state.player.name);
 const cacheKey=JSON.stringify([[...names].sort(),text]);if(data.cache.has(cacheKey))return data.cache.get(cacheKey);
 if(data.english.some(x=>text.includes(x.anchor)&&x.pattern.test(text)))return{status:'english',outputs:[text],ids:[]};
 const memo=new Map(),active=new Set();
 function resolve(value,depth){
  if(depth>5)return{status:'depth-limit',outputs:[],ids:[]};
  if(memo.has(value))return memo.get(value);if(active.has(value))return{status:'recursive',outputs:[],ids:[]};active.add(value);
  const outputs=new Set(),ids=new Set();let blocked=false,overflow=false;
  for(const row of data.exact.get(value)??[]){outputs.add(row.en);ids.add(row.id);}
  for(const rule of data.patterns){
   if(!value.includes(rule.anchor))continue;const match=rule.pattern.exec(value);if(!match)continue;
   const values=new Map();let good=true;
   for(let i=0;i<rule.tokens.length;i++){
    const key=rule.tokens[i],captured=match[i+1];let next;
    if(rule.row.identity.includes(key)){
     // Names can contain arbitrary punctuation. Only accept actual state names;
     // otherwise a leading name hole could consume an entire preceding paragraph.
     if(!names.has(captured)){good=false;blocked=true;break;}next=captured;
    }
    else if(names.has(captured)||!/[가-힣]/.test(captured))next=captured;
    else{const inner=resolve(captured,depth+1);if(inner.outputs.length!==1){good=false;blocked=true;break;}next=inner.outputs[0];}
    if(values.has(key)&&values.get(key)!==next){good=false;blocked=true;break;}values.set(key,next);
   }
   if(good){outputs.add(rule.row.en.replace(data.token,k=>values.get(k)??k));ids.add(rule.row.id);}
   if(outputs.size>12){overflow=true;break;}
  }
  let joined=false;
  if(outputs.size===0&&value.length>70)for(const rule of data.patterns){
   if(!rule.prefixes.length||!value.includes(rule.anchor))continue;
   for(const option of rule.prefixes){
    const hit=option.pattern.exec(value);if(!hit||hit[0].length>=value.length)continue;
    const left=resolve(hit[0],depth+1),right=resolve(value.slice(hit[0].length+option.join.separator.length),depth+1);
    if(left.status!=='matched'||right.status!=='matched'||!right.ids.some(id=>option.join.right.includes(data.owners.get(id))))continue;
    outputs.add(left.outputs[0]+option.join.separator+right.outputs[0]);for(const id of [...left.ids,...right.ids])ids.add(id);joined=true;
   }
  }
  if(joined)blocked=false;
  active.delete(value);
  const result={status:overflow?'ambiguous':blocked?'unresolved-template':outputs.size===1?'matched':outputs.size>1?'ambiguous':'unmatched',outputs:[...outputs],ids:[...ids]};memo.set(value,result);return result;
 }
 const out=resolve(text,0);if(data.cache.size>=512)data.cache.clear();data.cache.set(cacheKey,out);return out;
}
function koLogTone179(text,tone,state){
 if(tone&&Vse.has(tone))return tone;
 // Preserve English historical logs and arbitrary Latin user names verbatim.
 if(!/[가-힣]/.test(text))return koLogReference179(text,tone);
 const result=koLogResolve179(text,state);
 if(result.status==='matched'||result.status==='english')return koLogReference179(result.outputs[0],tone);
 return Kse(text,tone);
}

function koTail170(token) {
  const known=koNameTails170[token.toLowerCase()];
  if(known!==undefined) return known;
  const last=token.at(-1),code=last.charCodeAt(0);
  if(code>=44032&&code<=55203) return (code-44032)%28;
  if(/[0-9]/.test(last)) return [21,8,0,16,0,0,1,8,8,0][Number(last)];
  // Unknown Latin spellings use the final letter's Korean reading. This is a
  // display fallback, not a claim to infer every personal name's pronunciation.
  if(/[A-Za-z]/.test(last)) return /[lr]/i.test(last)?8:/[mn]/i.test(last)?4:0;
  return 0;
}
function koText(value) {
  if(typeof value==='string') return value.replace(/([\p{L}\p{N}_]+)([”’"'»\)\]\}」』》〉]*)(은\(는\)|는\(은\)|이\(가\)|가\(이\)|을\(를\)|를\(을\)|와\(과\)|과\(와\)|으로\(로\)|로\(으로\)|\(으\)로)/gu,(_,word,close,pair)=>{
    const tail=koTail170(word),choices=pair.includes('은')?['은','는']:pair.includes('을')?['을','를']:pair.includes('와')?['과','와']:(pair.includes('으로')||pair==='(으)로')?['으로','로']:['이','가'];
    return word+close+choices[tail&&(choices[0]!=='으로'||tail!==8)?0:1];
  });
  if(Array.isArray(value)) {
    let previous='';
    const visit=item=>{
      if(item==null||typeof item==='boolean')return item;
      if(Array.isArray(item)){
        const next=item.map(visit);
        return next.some((v,i)=>v!==item[i])?next:item;
      }
      if(typeof item==='string'||typeof item==='number'){
        const result=typeof item==='string'?koText(previous+item).slice(previous.length):item;
        // Keep only the phonetic suffix; empty/invisible children do not erase it.
        previous=(previous+String(result)).match(/[\p{L}\p{N}_]+[”’"'»\)\]\}」』》〉]*$/u)?.[0]??'';
        return result;
      }
      if(item&&typeof item==='object'&&(typeof item.type==='string'||item.type===runtime.Fragment)){
        if(['br','hr','img','input','textarea','select'].includes(item.type)){previous='';return item;}
        const children=visit(item.props?.children);
        if(children===item.props?.children)return item;
        // Preserve React keys, refs, owner, descriptors and all non-display props.
        const descriptors=Object.getOwnPropertyDescriptors(item);
        descriptors.props={...descriptors.props,value:{...item.props,children}};
        return Object.create(Object.getPrototypeOf(item),descriptors);
      }
      // A custom component's rendered text is unknown here.
      previous='';return item;
    };
    return visit(value);
  }
  return value;
}
